# Test Plan

## 1. Scope

- Requirement or release: frontend route/navigation baseline for an admin console.
- Affected routes/pages: `/`, `/my-work` redirect, `/my-work/overview`, `/my-work/tasks`, `/my-work/task-submit`, `/my-work/task-acceptance`, `/my-work/expense-collection`, `/my-work/expense-review`, `/my-work/archive`, `/my-work/salary`, `/my-work/performance`, `/people`, `/people/overview`, `/people/archives`, `/people/organization` redirect, `/people/movements`, `/people/expiry` legacy redirect, `/people/position-permissions`, `/people/capability` legacy redirect, `/people/attendance`, `/people/attendance-monthly`, `/people/roles` legacy redirect to `/system/roles`, `/customers` redirect, `/customers/archives`, `/customers/brands`, `/customers/stores`, `/customers/contracts`, `/performance` redirect, `/performance/dashboard`, `/performance/rules`, `/performance/signatures`, `/performance/monthly`, `/salary` redirect, `/salary/overview`, `/salary/monthly`, `/salary/social-insurance`, `/finance` redirect, `/finance/overview`, `/finance/business-overview`, `/finance/receivables-payables`, `/finance/transactions`, `/finance/review`, `/finance/account-ledger`, `/finance/bank-reconciliation`, `/finance/accounting-subjects`, `/finance/accounting-vouchers`, `/finance/general-ledger`, `/finance/period-close`, `/system/roles`, `/system/task-config`, `/system/work-calendar`, `/system/ledger-initialization`, `/system/finance-categories`, `*`.
- Affected API/data: no deployed API or persistent database implementation; current product behavior uses frontend-session state plus static mock data. Local API contract skeletons, where present, are non-runtime evidence only.
- Affected environments: local frontend app and local Docker Postgres baseline.
- Owner: TBD.

Current finance closed-period readback increment: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` must render closed-period pre-lifecycle rows as `只读回看 / 期间已结账` in the list `下一步` column while preserving `后续调整` for ordinary booked vouchers in closed periods. `/finance/general-ledger` must render only available close metadata in the read-only period-status section and must not show placeholder dashes for missing close time, operator, or reason.

Current finance closed-period source-write guard expectations: frontend-session source writes that can affect an accounting period must return an explicit success/failure result before any linked source state shows success. This includes cash-flow creation, receivable/payable generation, bank-reconciliation confirmation, invoice-ledger import, shared invoice association or rollback, red-original relation, declaration-preparation unbilled-income decisions, and ledger-initialization draft edits. The independent `发票匹配` route is removed; invoice association is shared by invoice ledger and receivable/payable detail drawers. When the affected accounting period is closed, the caller must stop before linked state changes and show readback/locked-period guidance. This is frontend-session guard behavior only; backend persistent period locks and source API mutation guards remain future backend evidence. `financePeriodCloseSourceMutationMatrix.test.ts` must keep the current source-write matrix aligned with owner pages, frontend write functions, accounting-period sources, local API route skeletons, and closed-period behavior.

Current month-end source expectation: `/finance/assets-prepayments` is the readable source ledger for fixed-asset depreciation and prepaid-expense amortization. `/finance/month-end` only consumes those schedules, keeps revenue confirmation as an `应收应付` readback when an accrual voucher already exists, and creates a voucher candidate only after the operator confirms a pending source matter. This confirmation is guarded by the frontend-session accounting-period guard; the backend source-mutation API, source ledger persistence, and source-write matrix entry are intentionally deferred until the accounting source tables are implemented.

Current salary closed-period source-write guard expectations: `/salary/monthly` salary-detail confirmation and salary-adjustment creation are payroll-result writes and use the salary attribution month as their accounting-period guard source. Actual salary payment is a separate fact: it uses the actual payment month as the guard source, so a closed salary-attribution month does not block a normal later-period payment. Every blocked write must stop before changing salary/payment state and show the same readback/locked-period guidance used by finance source modules. This remains frontend-session evidence only and is not backend payroll API lock evidence.

Current general-ledger opening-balance source increment: `/finance/general-ledger` keeps `期初来源` in the subject detail drawer, but visible source labels must be business-facing values such as `账套初始化`, `期初余额承接`, `期初导入`, or `前期已记账分录`; frontend model tests must prevent technical labels such as `后端期初余额` from reaching the page model.

Current period-close source-readiness boundary: `/finance/period-close` derives close checks from real owner-module readiness and accounting-voucher candidates, not an internal adjustment checklist. Payroll/social-insurance, tax handling, cash review, bank reconciliation, accounting vouchers, general ledger, and financial statements stay in their owner modules. The close drawer only summarizes blocking checks and does not expose source-module processing rows.

Current small-scale tax and month-end expectations: small-scale VAT must not inherit a global `1%` default. Receivable source facts remain valid before declaration, but their tax basis must show `待票面或申报判定` rather than fabricate a tax amount; invoice rates remain ticket facts and declaration preparation selects the filing-period candidate outcome. The tax-policy model must resolve one policy version by declaration accounting period, reject uncovered periods, preserve each invoice's invoice kind/taxable classification/VAT treatment, retain special-invoice VAT below the exemption threshold, calculate urban-maintenance tax, education surcharge, and local-education surcharge separately, and apply the six-tax-and-two-fee reduction only when the taxpayer-eligibility snapshot is `符合`. `/finance/month-end` is the upstream source workbench for fixed-asset depreciation, prepaid/deferred amortization, and contract acceptance revenue confirmation. A `待确认` month-end item blocks period close; confirming it creates a `待记账` voucher candidate, and only voucher booking posts it to the frontend-session general ledger. Inventory cost carryforward is out of current scope until inventory source facts exist.

Current period-close check-summary boundary expectation: `/finance/period-close` `结账检查` rows render only `检查项 / 结果 / 说明`. Source summaries remain in the page model for internal readiness and close snapshots, but they are not rendered as a drawer column and are not matched by keyword search.

Current period-close execution-track expectation: the active implementation round is `local_backend_contract_hardening`. Production framework/server integration, formal project schema migration/repository, real secret manager implementation, real DB client implementation, real DB runtime implementation, production service process pool wiring, production identity/session/cookie, production release, recovery, and audit archive evidence remain deferred scopes. `financePeriodCloseStageAuditMatrix.test.ts` must fail if current local contract evidence is rewritten as formal backend/database/production completion.

Rows before 2026-06-19 are historical execution records. Current personnel-module expectations follow the `Personnel boundary hardcut` result for archive/overview/movement boundaries and ADR-0005 for permissions: `/system/roles` is read-only `业务角色` default permission package display, while `岗位管理` binds business roles, owns position extra permissions, and previews effective permissions.

Current performance-detail expectations follow the 2026-06-22 `Performance detail action-boundary hardcut`: `/performance/monthly` is a read-only `绩效明细` result ledger, uses category tabs plus shared date-scope filtering, and does not expose `生成绩效明细`, create/edit/confirm/lock, row operation, or upload actions. Source corrections belong to source task/acceptance flows and are reflected back as read-only performance detail rows.

Current performance employment-scope expectations follow the 2026-06-23 `Performance employment-scope proration`: full-time personnel performance rows are adjusted by effective actual-workday ratio from joining/leaving date, personnel work calendar, and annual exceptions. Task-scoring raw task scores are not discounted; the full-score口径, base achievement line, base-performance cap, and excess-score bands are prorated. Duty-assessment amounts are prorated, project-bonus amounts keep actual confirmed values, and salary consumes the adjusted performance prepay rows.

Current performance execution-contract expectations follow the 2026-06-23 `Performance execution contract hardcut`: monthly performance result rows attach a compact execution contract from rule applicability, employee signed-file relationship, and monthly result version. `绩效明细`, `绩效看板`, and `薪酬明细` consume the contract-attached monthly rows; salary does not re-resolve performance rule/signature relationships.

Current salary-overview expectations follow the 2026-06-22 `Salary overview module`: `/salary/overview` is a read-only executive overview over salary detail rows, `/salary` redirects to it, and it does not expose create/edit/confirm/settle/generate/upload/payment actions. `薪酬统计` is no longer an active route or navigation module.

Current salary-social-insurance expectations follow the 2026-06-22 `Social-insurance consolidation hardcut`: `/salary/social-insurance` is the single active `社保公积金` route. It combines detail confirmation, official payment entry, payment-voucher upload, and linked payment cash-flow creation; detail confirmation is shown in the detail drawer `缴费明细` tab. The former separate payment route is no longer an active route or navigation module.

Current finance business-overview expectations follow the 2026-06-23 `Finance business overview module`, `Finance overview hardcut`, and `Finance business overview filter-first cleanup`, plus the 2026-06-24 handled-funds hardcut: `/finance/business-overview` is the read-only `经营总览` route and default finance landing surface. `/finance` and legacy `/finance/overview` redirect to it; `财务总览`, `财务统计`, and standalone handled-fund pages are no longer active navigation or page modules. The page puts the shared date-scope and view filter row as the first content layer, separates `经营确认` from `现金流水`, renders top operating result metrics with month-over-month and year-over-year comparisons, replaces the duplicated period-summary position with view-specific read-only operating status cards, and aggregates income/cost by first-level and second-level finance category without exposing source-row operations. The previously trialed `经营摘要` layer is removed because it duplicated the metric, status, and category-table facts. `经营确认` top metrics use `已确认收入 / 已确认成本 / 未收金额 / 未付金额` wording, and its status cards use only supported receivable/payable confirmation records: `确认事项 / 逾期应收 / 逾期应付 / 未收事项 / 未付事项`. `现金流水` status cards use only cash-side facts: `对公余额 / 待复核流水 / 未对账流水`. Rate metric comparisons display `-` when the previous comparable period has no income basis. Top metric, status, and table grids keep a consistent module spacing rhythm, and the top metric grid follows the active metric count so shorter metric sets do not clip amounts.

Current finance receivable-payable expectations follow the 2026-06-23 `Receivable-payable source-boundary hardcut` and `Receivable-payable settlement registration`, the 2026-06-24 `Receivable-payable settlement lifecycle`, `Receivable-payable payment-status hardcut`, and `Receivable-payable field-scope cleanup`, plus the 2026-06-28 `Receivable-payable accounting-period filter cleanup`: `/finance/receivables-payables` is the `应收应付` accrual confirmation ledger, not a source-document creation or confirmation-approval page. It uses shared workbench tabs that separate `未结清` and `已结清` confirmation items while keeping `方向` as the only `应收 / 应付` distinction. The `未结清` tab contains `未结清`, `待复核`, `部分结清`, and `部分待复核`; the `已结清` tab contains fully settled items only. The table has no extra toolbar title, derived `收付状态` placed near the leading identity columns, shared `DateScopeFilter` for `归属月份`, restrained filters for direction/keyword plus active-tab收付状态 filtering, neutral list amount columns, and row detail drawers for事项信息, ordinary public-account collection/payment registration with linked cash-flow rows in the progress section, tax-basis summary, linked invoice/bill rows, confirmation-basis files, and operation records. The `发票/票据` detail tab must show read-only `税额依据` before linked invoice rows so users can distinguish source tax basis from invoice-ledger evidence. The page does not expose `新增应收/应付`, ledger-local manual registration, ledger-local confirmation action, upstream confirmation-status filter, or confirmation-status list/detail fields. Receivable/payable rows must come from upstream business sources such as contracts, project-stage confirmation, salary, monthly or quarterly performance, outsourcing settlement, employee reimbursement approval, procurement, or operating-expense source modules after those source modules already confirmed the fact; pending upstream confirmations do not enter the page list, invoice-matching candidates, or accounting-voucher candidates. Salary and monthly performance due dates are the 15th of the month after the accounting month; quarterly performance is paid with the salary for the month containing the quarterly settlement date. `调整事项` works only before the confirmation item has settled progress and before any active receipt/payment cash-flow row exists, and it cannot directly edit source-owned counterparty fields, settled amount, or derived收付状态. Active settlement rows are `待复核` or `已入账`; `退回修改` and `已作废` rows remain history but no longer occupy registerable amount or lock a zero-settled confirmation item. `未结清` and `部分结清` can register receipt/payment rows up to `未结清金额 - 待复核流水金额`; rows fully occupied by pending-review cash flow show `待复核` and cannot register more until the cash flow is reviewed or returned; `已结清` cannot register again. Collection/payment registration requires at least one receipt/payment voucher, creates a `待复核` cash-flow row, and links the transaction number back to the receivable/payable item. `流水复核`入账 writes the settled amount and settlement status back to the linked receivable/payable item. `已逾期` is not a receivable/payable primary status; overdue is derived from due date plus outstanding amount and appears as a compact red `逾期` tag next to `收付状态` in the list. Confirmation-basis files use the shared related-file table with preview/download actions. The receivable/payable page and `经营总览` consume the same frontend-session confirmed rows from `FinanceManagementState`; reimbursement payment stays in this settlement path, while employee collection enters `/finance/review` from `/my-work/expense-collection` after company-account transfer proof is complete.

Current finance bank-reconciliation expectations follow the 2026-06-24 `Bank reconciliation workbench refactor`, `Bank reconciliation date/status refinement`, `Bank reconciliation import guidance`, `Bank reconciliation import precheck`, the 2026-06-25 `Bank reconciliation import contract`, the 2026-06-26 quick-handling refinement, and the 2026-06-27 multi-public-account hardcut: `/finance/bank-reconciliation` is a restrained bank-statement import and reconciliation to-do workbench. The main page has top metrics `待处理 / 待确认 / 异常 / 已对账`, a primary `导入银行流水` entry, a left compact `对账待办` queue, and a right detail panel. It intentionally has no date/status/keyword filters, no table tabs, no bank-pool/report tables, and no detail drawer. The import drawer owns selected public account, bank-statement date range, file requirement copy, upload selection, import precheck, and import preview; imported batch identity is derived from the statement dates rather than the current system month. Import is free-form for the selected account and not blocked by pending system rows, while the import card and drawer provide suggested supplement periods from reviewed-but-unreconciled public-account flows for that account and show which suggested periods the preview covers. `financeBankImportContract.ts` owns the frontend import field contract: required fields are `账户名称 / 交易日期 / 银行流水号 / 收支方向 / 交易金额 / 对方户名 / 摘要`, and duplicate detection uses `publicAccountId + bankSerialNo` so the same serial number on a different account is not treated as the same bank statement row and mutable display names do not define identity. Import precheck verifies selected-account match, required fields, duplicate unique keys from existing rows or the same file, date coverage, and importable rows. A mixed-account file is rejected in the first frontend version through account-mismatch blocking rows. Account or required-field failures block import; duplicate rows are skipped; matching or amount exceptions enter the reconciliation to-do pool for later handling. Displayed statuses are limited to `待处理 / 待确认 / 异常 / 已对账`, while internal causes stay in handling-result copy and explanatory copy. Selecting a queue row shows bank summary facts, system period, bank/system date relationship, `对账判断`, compact same-account `可匹配系统流水`, matched system-flow details, system voucher preview/download, and operation records. The pending queue is not scoped to the current month; historical reviewed-but-unreconciled system cash flows, such as a May row, remain eligible for matching and confirmation. Quick handling is limited to前置处理：`补登记系统流水` creates a `待复核 / 未对账` public-account cash-flow row from an unmatched bank statement and does not bypass `/finance/review`; `补充银行流水` opens the import drawer for system-side rows missing bank evidence; `查看复核进度` shows only the linked pending-review flow. `确认对账` remains the final reconciliation operation and is disabled unless the matched system transaction is入账、方向一致、金额一致、同一对公账户且未被对账；撤销对账 is not currently exposed on this page.

Current finance accounting-voucher expectations follow the 2026-06-27 `Finance accounting voucher status hardcut`, `Finance accounting voucher invoice-tax integration`, `Finance accounting voucher bank-reconciliation prerequisite`, `Finance accounting voucher tax-matter integration`, `Finance accounting voucher lifecycle and backend contract`, plus the 2026-06-28 `Finance invoice business-source hardcut`, `Finance accrual tax-basis hardcut`, `Finance accounting voucher prepare/book split`, `Finance accrual readiness frontload`, `Finance tax-policy profile hardcut`, `Finance tax/accounting backend contract`, and the 2026-06-29 `Finance funds-voucher phase 1 backend contract`: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` is the accounting-voucher booking workbench with a frontend-session lifecycle, not a persisted backend period-close or audit-grade ledger system. Confirmed and frontloaded-ready `/finance/receivables-payables` rows derive `权责凭证` booking matters from the accrual fact; rows missing source facts, original evidence, tax basis, or accounting-subject mapping stay in the source readiness flow and do not become accrual voucher issue rows. `/tax/invoices` facts matched through business/accrual source refs provide invoice evidence and can provide the preferred tax basis when fully matched. Shared `financeTaxPolicyModel` owns the frontend prototype taxpayer type, VAT calculation/input-treatment, declaration-threshold, and CIT preparation parameters consumed by source-tax-basis, invoice matching, declaration preparation, and `/system/tax-policy`; imported invoice rows remain external invoice facts. Backend cutover must replace that prototype model with `tax_policy_profiles`, `invoice_tax_facts`, `receivable_payable_tax_facts`, `finance_category_accounting_mappings`, and active `accounting_subjects` as defined in `docs/data/finance-tax-accounting-backend-contract.md`. Reviewed and bank-reconciled `/finance/transactions` rows derive `资金凭证` booking matters; confirmed `/tax/handling` tax matters with payable amount derive `税费凭证` booking matters; reviewed and bank-reconciled `流水冲正 / 流水补差` rows, `/system/ledger-initialization` opening-adjustment rows, plus unresolved red invoices derive `调整凭证` handling rows. Opening-adjustment vouchers use source module `账套初始化`, the adjusted subject on the selected debit/credit side, and the opposite side to `3104 利润分配` in the current frontend prototype. Ordinary blue invoices no longer match ordinary cash-flow rows directly for accrual recognition; cash-flow refs belong to资金凭证, while `后续开票待处理` may still reference a historical未开票收入流水 as declaration evidence. Reviewed but unreconciled cash-flow or adjustment rows remain `待生成`; confirmed but unfinished tax matters remain `待生成`; bank-reconciliation abnormal rows, unresolved red invoices, and completed tax matters missing required receipts become `需处理`. Status values are `待生成 / 需处理 / 待记账 / 已记账 / 已冲销`; current frontend derivation emits `待记账` for complete balanced rows and `需处理` for non-accrual blocking issues such as invalid amount, unbalanced entries, red invoice without its original blue-invoice relation, bank amount mismatch, or missing tax receipts. Receivable accrual vouchers split resolved tax basis into `应交税费`: fully matched output invoices are preferred, while partial or missing output invoices fall back to receivable/payable source tax basis and remain visible as invoice evidence status rather than blocking accrual generation. Payable accrual vouchers retain matched input invoices as cost evidence but do not create VAT input-deduction entries under the current small-scale taxpayer profile; tax-accrual vouchers credit `应交税费` while linked tax-payment cash-flow rows remain separate `资金凭证` after review and bank reconciliation. The page has status metrics, shared date-scope filtering, status/keyword filters, and workbench table columns for voucher-matter identity, business step, status, prerequisite summary, next step, accounting period, counterparty, and amount. It intentionally does not show a business-type filter or debit/credit subject columns in the table. Pre-lifecycle rows use the source document number as their primary identity; only `已记账 / 已冲销` rows show the session `记-*` voucher number. The drawer uses `依据 / 分录 / 记录` tabs: `依据` contains source, tax basis, invoice evidence, and prerequisites; `分录` contains pre-lifecycle preview or session entries plus `记账影响` after booking; `记录` appears only after lifecycle operations exist. Line rows preserve missing subject codes instead of falling back to a default administrative-expense subject. The drawer can simulate `记账` and `冲销` in frontend session state; simulated记账 assigns a mock session voucher number, records `记账人 / 记账时间`, freezes a session-local voucher snapshot, and derives `记账影响` from that snapshot; simulated冲销 now requires a reversal-confirmation drawer with reversal date and reason, then marks the original booked snapshot `已冲销` while creating a linked booked `冲销凭证` snapshot with opposite entries, so original historical entries remain and the reversal voucher offsets them. For a row whose accounting period is already locked, the drawer shows a closed-period warning explaining why记账或原期间冲销 is blocked. For a booked voucher whose original accounting period is already locked, the drawer switches from same-period冲销 to `后续调整`: the adjustment drawer requires target adjustment period, adjustment date, and reason; it creates a target-period `调整凭证` candidate with opposite entries and an `调整依据` reference to the original voucher, while the original voucher remains `已记账` and unchanged. The frontend lifecycle does not expose a separate voucher approval/review status. Backend integration must write `记账人 / 记账时间 / 冲销人 / 冲销时间` from authenticated account snapshots and server time; frontend clients must not submit or override those actor/time fields. Period-lock checks are consumed by this page and by `/finance/period-close`, but persistence remains frontend-session only until backend accounting-period APIs exist. Backend cutover contracts and remaining readiness evidence requirements are documented in `docs/data/finance-funds-voucher-phase1-backend-contract.md`, `docs/data/finance-tax-accounting-backend-contract.md`, and `docs/data/finance-accounting-voucher-ledger-contract.md`, where cash facts, tax/accounting facts, candidates, persisted vouchers, voucher-number sequences, booking, reversal, later-period adjustment, period locks, and general-ledger API contracts are separated.

Current accounting-voucher tax-period expectation: tax-accrual voucher candidates use the tax matter's mapped statutory/business period as `accountingPeriod`, while linked tax-payment cash-flow rows remain separate funds-voucher candidates in the actual payment transaction period. Cross-period tax fixtures must not collapse accrual and payment facts into the same accounting period unless the statutory period and payment month are actually the same.

Current accounting-voucher closed-period readback expectation: when a historical `已冲销` original or linked `冲销凭证` belongs to a closed frontend-session accounting period, its detail warning must use readback-only copy instead of a generic action-blocking prompt. These rows are historical voucher facts and should not imply further same-period processing.

Current system ledger-initialization expectations follow the 2026-06-30 `Finance account-set initialization and small-enterprise subject hardcut` plus the current initialization workbench hardcut: `/system/ledger-initialization` is the one-off account-set initialization workbench under `系统管理`, not a finance result table. The page must render workflow steps `基础设置 / 科目确认 / 期初余额 / 试算平衡 / 启用账套`, metrics `启用期间 / 账套状态 / 流程进度 / 期初借方 / 期初贷方 / 试算差额`, and internal views `基础设置 / 科目确认 / 期初余额 / 试算结果 / 启用记录`. The frontend page separates `初始化草稿` and `已启用账套`: draft mode is the default testing workspace and keeps base settings, opening-balance entry, import preview, trial calculation, import application, enablement, and immediate unused enablement withdrawal actions available; enabled mode is the locked snapshot that explains downstream use and disables direct editing. The model statuses are `未开始 / 基础设置完成 / 科目已确认 / 期初已录入 / 试算平衡 / 试算不平 / 已启用`; trial imbalance must resolve `试算不平`, block enablement, and keep total-ledger opening balances out of an enabled/locked state. The `期初余额` view has nested views `录入台账 / 导入预览 / 校验结果`: `录入台账` keeps opening-balance filters and row drawer, `导入预览` shows imported rows before application, and `校验结果` shows blocking/non-blocking validation items. Model tests must cover draft editability, locked enabled account sets, import preview application into the full opening-balance draft, `录入方式` keyword filtering, blocking validation issues such as missing subject, invalid debit/credit direction, duplicate rows, and trial-balance difference, and the rule that only enabled balanced snapshots feed general-ledger opening balances. `科目确认` reads the same small-enterprise accounting-subject catalog as `/finance/accounting-subjects`; `启用记录` shows operator/time snapshots, `期初来源: 账套初始化`, `账簿使用`, `调整凭证`, and post-enablement controlled actions for `撤回启用` and `期初调整`. `撤回启用` is executable only before downstream vouchers, period-close locks, or ledger usage exists and returns the account set to `试算平衡`; after downstream dependencies exist, corrections must use `期初调整`, which creates a `待记账` opening-adjustment voucher record rather than rewriting the original opening rows. That adjustment is shared into `/finance/accounting-vouchers`; after frontend-session记账, it contributes normal booked ledger entries while the original enabled opening balance remains unchanged. When the frontend-session effective period is already closed, draft enablement is disabled, post-enablement handling shows readback-only copy, and both withdrawal plus opening-adjustment actions are restricted. This remains frontend mock context until backend account-set, opening-balance import/manual-entry, trial-balance, lock, opening-adjustment, accounting-voucher, and general-ledger APIs exist.

Current finance general-ledger expectations follow the 2026-06-29 `Finance general ledger frontend` plus the current period-boundary refinement, balance-side display refinement, and account-set initialization hardcut: `/finance/general-ledger` is a read-only `总账` result page after accounting-voucher booking. It consumes frontend-session booked voucher facts: ordinary `已记账` vouchers, `账套初始化` opening-adjustment vouchers after记账, booked later-period adjustment vouchers after记账, `已冲销` original vouchers whose historical entries remain, and linked booked `冲销凭证` rows whose opposite entries offset the original. `待生成 / 需处理 / 待记账` rows must not affect balances. The page uses shared `DateScopeFilter` with `会计期间`, but this subject-balance surface exposes only `当月 / 按月份` and defaults to the current mock month `2026-06`; filtering is by voucher `accountingPeriod`, not voucher operation time, cash-flow review time, or bank reconciliation time. `/system/ledger-initialization` supplies frontend opening balances only from the enabled and balanced account-set snapshot; initialization drafts do not feed general ledger, and opening adjustments do not rewrite the enabled snapshot. Earlier booked entries roll into `期初余额`; selected-month entries become `本期借方 / 本期贷方`; `期末余额` uses the subject balance direction formula while retaining signed model balances and displaying the actual debit/credit balance side plus absolute amount. Metrics are period-level `会计期间 / 期间状态 / 试算状态 / 已记账凭证 / 本期借方 / 本期贷方` before科目类别/关键字 filtering; filters are `会计期间 / 科目类别 / 关键字`; table columns are `科目 / 类别 / 正常余额 / 期初余额 / 本期借方 / 本期贷方 / 期末余额 / 凭证数`. Clicking a row opens a read-only `总账明细` drawer with summary cards, `期间状态`, `科目信息`, and `科目明细`; `期间状态` repeats the selected period, frontend-session lock state, trial-balance status, and closed-period close reason when available, while `科目信息` includes `期初来源`. No create/edit/book/reverse/period-close action is exposed in this page. Account-set profile, opening balances, opening adjustments, and period locks are frontend mock context only until backend account-set initialization, opening-balance, accounting-period, and general-ledger APIs exist.

Current general-ledger closed-period readback expectation: when the selected accounting period is `已结账`, the subject detail drawer must show a readback-only alert in `期间状态` before the close metadata, because the page is a result view and must not imply ledger editing or source correction in the closed period.

Current finance period-close expectations follow the 2026-07-01 `Finance period close frontend` and the current formal-statement/source-readiness hardcut: `/finance/period-close` is the restrained month-end close workbench after `/finance/general-ledger`. It derives one row per accounting period from frontend-session accounting-voucher rows, including later-period adjustment records, booked ledger entries, pending-review and reviewed cash-flow rows, tax-handling matters, salary-social-insurance matters, financial-statement readiness, and period locks. Quarterly or annual tax-handling periods are mapped to their closing accounting month, such as `2026-Q2 -> 2026-06`, and must not create standalone accounting-period rows. The page exposes metrics `期间数 / 可结账 / 需处理 / 已结账`, filters `会计期间`, `结账状态`, and keyword, and shows table columns `会计期间 / 结账状态 / 凭证处理 / 总账试算 / 报表 / 资金核对 / 税务社保 / 本期借贷 / 结账信息`. `资金核对` separates `待复核` cash-flow rows from reviewed-but-unreconciled cash-flow rows; either one can block close. `结账信息` shows close time/operator for closed periods, `待确认结账 / 检查已通过` for closeable periods, and `待完成检查 / N 项待处理` for pending periods; it is read-only and not an operation column. `结账状态` is a model-level filter for `全部 / 可结账 / 需处理 / 已结账`; keyword remains for period and check text search, not as the primary way to narrow close status. Clicking a row opens a detail drawer with two tabs: `结账检查` and `结账信息`. `结账检查` contains only the close-blocking check table. `结账信息` contains result sections only: `报表状态`, conditional `待处理事项`, conditional `后续调整`, and a result section. Open or pending periods show `期间汇总` with accounting period, debit/credit totals, and trial difference, while closed periods show `结账结果` with close operator, close time, and close reason. Duplicated close status and trial-balance status stay in the drawer title and summary cards instead of being repeated in the grid. `待处理事项` renders only non-zero blockers; `后续调整` renders only when historical-period adjustment or current-period carryover counts are non-zero. The detail summary cards use a state-aware check summary: pending periods show `待处理检查`, closeable periods show `结账检查 N 项通过`, and closed periods show `结账检查 N 项回看`. Checks cover voucher source readiness, voucher booking, general-ledger trial balance, financial-statement readiness, cash-flow review, bank reconciliation, tax-handling completion, and social-insurance/housing-fund completion. Open-period check rows show only `检查项 / 结果 / 说明`; they do not expose `检查依据`, concrete voucher numbers, transaction numbers, tax matter numbers, social-insurance matter numbers, `处理建议`, `回看说明`, or source-route action columns inside the drawer. Operators read `结果` and `说明`, then return to the owning module from navigation when correction is needed. `报表状态` distinguishes `待结账 / 正式报表 / 暂不可出具`; cash-flow statement mapping gaps are close blockers through the financial-statement readiness check. `确认结账` appears only when all checks pass and opens a confirmation drawer; the drawer requires `结账说明`, does not repeat the status field already shown in the drawer title, shows `结账检查` passed count instead of a redundant zero阻断项, scopes the confirmation warning to read-only period readback, and states that later-period adjustment is the normal correction path while reopen is exceptional. It then writes a frontend-session `AccountingPeriodLockRecord` with close operator, close time, close reason, close-time `closeCheckSnapshot`, and `closeSummarySnapshot`. Closed-period check rows remain visible for historical readback and prefer the stored check snapshot so later frontend source-state drift does not replace the close-time check detail/source summary. Summary cards, list columns, result-section totals, financial-statement rows, and cash-flow pending counts prefer the stored summary snapshot for the same reason. Closed-period check rows stay read-only and do not add source-handling guidance, source-route actions, or a `回看说明` column. Repeated frontend close attempts for the same already-closed accounting period must preserve the first close actor, close time, close reason, close-check snapshot, and close-summary snapshot instead of overwriting the close record; an `已反结账` frontend status may be replaced by a later `已结账` session record when the period is closed again. That frontend-session lock is consumed by accounting-voucher prepare/book/reversal guards, general-ledger period status, and the main source mutation guards for receivable/payable adjustment and settlement registration, invoice matching, cash-flow review, bank reconciliation confirmation, tax-payment cash-flow creation, reimbursement approval into payable, employee collection submission, cash-flow correction creation, ledger-initialization opening-adjustment voucher creation, and accounting-voucher later-period adjustment creation. The page does not expose reopen/reverse-close, source mutation, voucher editing, bank matching, tax handling, social-insurance payment, fixed-asset depreciation, amortization calculation, or report editing. This remains a frontend mock lock until backend accounting-period close checks and lock APIs are implemented.

Historical finance period-close backend contract prototype notes are retained only to document earlier exploratory contract work and are not part of the frontend-only execution scope. The 2026-07-02 frontend scope was frontend-session period-close presentation, guards, and evidence; API route adapters, database persistence, auth middleware, SQL migrations, real repository transactions, and source-service integration were not gates for that frontend round. The 2026-07-03 local API authorization increment reopens only the local framework-agnostic contract skeleton under `apps/api`: `corepack pnpm --filter @startupmanage/api test` is required for that increment, but it still does not prove real auth middleware, real HTTP routes, database persistence, or backend readiness.

Current voucher and period-close wording-boundary increment: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` must treat later-period corrections as creating a follow-up adjustment matter that enters the normal待记账 lifecycle, not as directly directly generating an already-prepared adjustment voucher. Cash-flow-derived voucher summaries should use collection/payment/review-confirmation wording such as `收款确认`, `付款确认`, and `补差确认` rather than cash-ledger `入账` wording. `/finance/period-close` funds-reconciliation checks should describe `已复核现金流水` completion of bank reconciliation, keeping cash-flow review distinct from accounting-voucher记账 and general-ledger posting.

Current accounting-voucher booking copy increment: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` must keep the `待记账 -> 记账` next-step copy business-facing and frontend-scope-safe. The list should describe booking as `确认分录入总账`, and lifecycle operation records should state that voucher entries were recorded, without claiming backend persistence, audit-grade ledger posting, or official period close.

Current accounting-voucher prerequisite copy increment: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` `需处理` rows must describe actual accounting blockers, not source-module operation entries. `未对账` alone is a period-close blocker and must not appear as a funds-voucher prerequisite; only bank amount discrepancies or missing confirmed bank-link evidence remain bank-related voucher blockers. The list `下一步` and drawer `前置条件` copy should use wording such as `需处理银行对账差异`, `需补齐银行对账依据`, `需补齐税费回执`, `需确认发票或来源税额`, and `需补齐来源凭证`; it must not expose source-module jump-back style copy as if the accounting-voucher page provides direct routing or source mutation actions.

Current voucher-close business copy increment: `/finance/accounting-vouchers` operation records and `/finance/period-close` closed-period readback copy must stay frontend-scope-safe. Accounting-voucher lifecycle records should use restrained copy such as `确认分录入总账` instead of implying official backend voucher generation, and closed-period close-check alerts should describe historical readback and same-period direct-handling restrictions without source-module handling wording.

Current general-ledger readback notice increment: `/finance/general-ledger` must make closed-period readback visible at page level, not only after opening a subject drawer. When the selected accounting period is `已结账`, the page should show a restrained notice that the ledger is historical readback and corrections should go through later-period adjustment or exceptional reopen flow; this remains frontend-session state and must not claim backend ledger or period-lock persistence.

Current accounting-voucher later-period adjustment backend expectations follow the 2026-07-02 contract-status review in `docs/api/finance-period-close-api-contract.md` and `docs/data/finance-accounting-voucher-ledger-contract.md`. The backend test matrix must prove that a closed-period booked original voucher plus a later unlocked target period creates a later-period adjustment source fact and target-period `待记账` `调整凭证` candidate; the original voucher remains `已记账` without `已冲销`, `reversed_by_voucher_id`, line changes, or historical ledger rewrites; a locked or not-later target period rejects without creating the source fact; the candidate does not affect target-period general-ledger totals until prepared and booked; and idempotency/version/permission failures leave both original voucher and adjustment facts unchanged. `apps/api/src/finance/accountingVoucherLaterPeriodAdjustmentContract.ts` now provides a local framework-agnostic contract/domain and route skeleton, and `corepack pnpm --filter @startupmanage/api test` covers source fact/candidate creation, original-voucher immutability, idempotent replay, reused-key rejection, open-original-period rejection, invalid or closed target-period rejection, non-booked/reversal/already-reversed original rejection, route-owned actor/time injection, state updates, invalid body, missing voucher, and unknown route behavior. This remains partial backend evidence only: it has no listening HTTP server, database persistence, auth middleware, transaction boundary, route-level permission enforcement for this endpoint, or target-period general-ledger integration tests.

Current handled-fund hardcut expectations follow the 2026-06-24 `Expense collection draft and handled-funds removal` change: `/finance/handled-funds` and `/finance/fund-handling` are removed routes with no active sidebar entry. `员工代收 / 现金暂收` no longer use an intermediate handled-fund workbench. Employee-facing collection can be saved as draft until the company public account and transfer/deposit proof are complete; final submission creates a linked `待复核` public-account cash-flow row that is reviewed in `/finance/review`.

Current my-work expense-collection expectations follow the 2026-06-23 `My work expense and collection module` plus the 2026-06-24 draft/hardcut change: `/my-work/expense-collection` is the employee-facing `费用与代收` route. It combines two document types in one table: `费用报销` for employee-advanced expenses and `代收登记` for customer money already transferred or deposited to the company public account before final submission. The page uses shared date-scope filtering plus restrained type/status/keyword filters, a primary `新增` form drawer, reusable `FileSlot` voucher upload, and a read-only detail drawer with `事项信息 / 凭证附件 / 处理记录`. The drawer supports `保存草稿`; final reimbursement submission enters `待审核`, while final collection submission requires target public account plus transfer proof and creates a linked `待复核` cash-flow row for `/finance/review`. Expense reimbursement selects finance-configured first-level and second-level employee-advance business categories, while paper-original handling status is read-only audit/finance processing output and is not editable by the submitter. It must not expose accounting entries, generation mode, settlement ids, or finance approval actions. It does not pay employees, settle receivables directly, review transactions, reconcile bank statements, or post vouchers; those remain finance-module responsibilities.

Current my-work expense-review expectations include the 2026-07-13 natural-person-payment hard cut: `/my-work/expense-review` remains the reviewer-side workbench for `费用报销` documents only and adds no approval step. The existing `通过` action must save the three conclusions `费用真实性 / 凭证合规性 / 超标准性质` and conserve the submitted amount across `业务报销 / 转薪酬计税 / 员工承担`. It creates an employee payable only for the business-reimbursement amount, transfers the taxable amount once to the identified employee's salary adjustment, and creates no payment cash flow or accounting voucher directly. Missing stable employee id blocks taxable transfer. `退回修改` still requires a reason.

Current natural-person-payment expectations follow `docs/governance/natural-person-payment-iit-hardcut-plan-v1.md`: performance has salary as its only payment entry; resident individual labor agreements are gross amounts; a 3,200 yuan labor batch derives 480 yuan withheld IIT and 2,720 yuan bank payment; pending and posted settlement capacity use the 3,200 yuan gross payable; the cash voucher splits gross payable into bank net plus `2221.03`; and IIT preparation includes posted labor facts, blocks unmatched posted salary/labor payments, and removes an origin only after its reversal is posted. The registered Web unit suite covers these cross-module invariants.

Current finance transaction closed-period expectations follow the 2026-07-02 frontend-only scope correction: `/finance/transactions` remains a cash-flow ledger and should not imply backend period-lock enforcement. For frontend-session closed periods, an `已入账` row detail renders a readback-only notice and hides the `冲正补差` drawer entry; direct adjustment submission still rechecks the target adjustment period before creating a pending-review adjustment row. For frontend-only changes, `test:api` is not required; for any `apps/api` local contract change, `corepack pnpm test:api` is required. Root `typecheck` now covers both web and the local API contract package.

Current tax declaration-preparation closed-period expectations follow the same frontend-session correction: `/tax/declaration-preparation` maps monthly, quarterly, and annual preparation periods to the accounting period that can be locked (`2026-Q2` maps to `2026-06`; annual labels map to December). For frontend-session closed periods, the detail drawer must show readback-only copy, replace item-level confirmation actions with `只读回看`, disable unbilled-income confirm/defer actions, and block `确认准备结果`. `financeTaxMatterModel.test.ts` covers the period mapping helper. Local API contract coverage now also treats declaration-preparation item confirmation, unbilled-income decision, subsequent-invoice handling, and preparation-result confirmation as `tax_handling` source mutations that must be blocked before source rows change when the accounting period is closed. Real backend persisted tax-preparation mutation guards remain future backend evidence.

Current tax/social handling closed-period expectations follow the same frontend-only correction: `/tax/handling` and the shared salary-social-insurance handling drawer use the shared business-period-to-accounting-period helper before exposing operations. For frontend-session closed periods, the detail drawer must show readback-only copy, hide official-handling and receipt-upload actions, block receipt save/payment-row generation in the handler path, and render social-insurance employee detail actions read-only. Backend persisted tax/social mutation guards are not current gates in this frontend round.

Current tax-handling evidence-boundary expectation: `/tax/handling` derives the `待补回执` metric and action from declaration evidence readiness rather than the composite next-action status. A filed item may remain pending payment or refund while also exposing missing declaration evidence. `补充申报回执` uses a dedicated drawer and domain command that can only add a declaration receipt file or receipt number; it must preserve the official declaration result, payable/refundable amount, settlement direction, and declaration version. Changing those facts remains an explicit declaration revision flow.

Current period-close voucher-count contract expectation: local API contract tests must cover `GET /api/finance/accounting-periods` returning non-zero `bookedVoucherCount / unbookedVoucherCount` from local voucher lifecycle rows grouped by `accountingPeriod + status`. `bookedVoucherCount` includes `已记账` and `已冲销`, and `unbookedVoucherCount` is `待记账`. The same local voucher row source must also drive `GET /api/finance/accounting-periods/{period}/close-checks` `voucher_booking` blockers and `POST /api/finance/accounting-periods/{period}/close` rejection when unbooked vouchers remain. Fixed zero values, frontend recomputation, or list/check/close source drift are not acceptable evidence. This remains a local contract expectation until a real voucher-ledger repository query exists.

Current finance settlement-correction expectation: a linked `已记账` or `已冲销` accrual voucher locks the original confirmation item against direct editing. Corrections enter the existing voucher reversal, red-letter/difference correction, or later-period adjustment lifecycle. The receivable/payable settled amount is derived from the net of linked `已入账` receipt/payment rows, tracing posted reversal and variance rows back to the original settlement. Pending review rows reserve registration capacity only, and returned or voided rows reserve none. A posted reversal of a settled receipt/payment must automatically reopen the underlying confirmation item.

## 3. Fixtures And Data

- Current frontend baseline uses static plaintext mock personnel archive data in `apps/web/src/features/people/mockData.ts`; identity, contact, address, contract, emergency contact, payment profile, and salary archive examples are fake prototype values and are not real personal data.
- Current personnel organization baseline also uses frontend-session attendance/leave records in `apps/web/src/features/people/PeopleOrganizationState.tsx`; these records are prototype availability facts only and are not punch-clock, payroll deduction, OA approval, or monthly attendance-report evidence.
- Current task-list assignment fixtures may read personnel attendance/leave records together with each person's applicable work calendar and the frontend built-in annual holiday/makeup-workday rules for frontend-only assignment candidate checks and expected-delivery derivation. That consumption belongs to the task module test scope.
- Current customer-management baseline uses static mock customer, brand, store, and lightweight contract data in `apps/web/src/features/customers/customerData.ts`; these records are prototype reference data only and are not a data contract.
- Current performance baseline uses static mock rule presets and drawing-task monthly performance summary rows in `apps/web/src/features/performance/performanceMockData.ts`, plus shared employee performance-rule file association mock data in `apps/web/src/features/performance/performanceSignatureArchiveData.ts`; the only detailed published rule is the space-design position rule, performance signing is represented as uploaded offline-signed files split into current signed files and historical files, and monthly performance rows are read-only employee-month-rule-version summary examples rather than source-task detail rows or payroll execution results.
- Current finance baseline uses static mock transaction records, category configuration, and frontend-local category snapshots in `apps/web/src/features/finance/financeData.ts`, static receivable/payable confirmation records in `apps/web/src/features/finance/financeReceivablesPayablesData.ts`, plus active-session category edits through `apps/web/src/features/system/FinanceCategoryState.tsx`; these rows are prototype data only and are not an accounting subject ledger, API contract, or auditable finance record.
- Current finance baseline uses static mock operating cash-flow transaction rows in `apps/web/src/features/finance/financeData.ts`; these rows are frontend-only examples and are not bank records, accounting vouchers, payment instructions, payroll execution evidence, invoice records, or auditable finance records.
- Mock data is not a contract and must not be treated as production data.
- Future fixtures should include customer/brand/store/task/rule/personnel/settlement/finance examples after module boundaries are confirmed.
- Seed data: TBD.
- External service policy: TBD.
- Privacy constraints: pending real data inventory and API model.

## 8. Residual Risk

- Covered in this thread: frontend model/unit gates and targeted browser smoke for the main voucher-ledger-period-close flow and period-close source-route behavior.
- Not covered: committed visual-regression artifacts, accessibility scan, full E2E suite, real backend/API/DB/Auth/Security behavior, persisted period locks, source API mutation guards, or production/release readiness.
- Reason: current active scope is frontend-session period-close workflow only; backend/local API skeletons are contract evidence, not runtime readiness.
- Risk: browser coverage is targeted rather than exhaustive, and frontend-session locks remain non-authoritative after refresh, multi-user access, or direct API calls.
- Follow-up: after frontend entry guards are stable, move to real backend close-checks, persisted accounting-period locks, source mutation API guards, and accessibility/visual regression gates.

## 9. Stamp Duty Hardcut Acceptance

- Unit coverage must prove: tax-item classification never guesses uncertain
  consulting/property-transfer facts; separately stated VAT is excluded from
  basis; six-tax/two-fee reduction uses the period policy; open-amount documents
  produce independent later settlement events; supplemental agreements use only
  the delta; capital books tax only increases over the historical taxed basis;
  duplicate source-version-item keys are rejected.
- Integration coverage must prove: signed documents and manual source documents
  enter one preparation; confirmed preparation drives tax handling payable
  amount; stamp-duty tax handling creates `5403 / 2221.05`; payment creates
  `2221.05 / 1002`; no receivable/payable row is created.
- UI coverage must prove: contract forms collect business facts but expose no tax
  rate or tax amount; stamp-duty preparation uses the shared `申报概览 / 申报明细 / 操作记录`
  drawer structure, with tax-source rows only in `申报明细` and audit history only in `操作记录`; manual source input accepts document facts and evidence only; tax
  handling exposes no manual amount editor; tax-policy details show actual
  document/capital-book filing cycles and the legal item table.
- Required gates: `corepack pnpm typecheck`, `corepack pnpm lint`,
  `corepack pnpm test:web`, `corepack pnpm build:web`,
  `corepack pnpm ai-native:check`, and `git diff --check`.
- Source-resolution coverage must additionally prove: pending sources have no
  default tax item; taxable/non-taxable/offset conclusions preserve actor,
  date, reason, source version, and obligation period; every multi-item or
  decrease allocation equals the complete source basis; capital-book sources
  read `3001 / 3002` general-ledger balances instead of page constants.

## 10. Tax Handling Settlement Acceptance

- Filing results use `申报成功 / 零申报` and separately persist the settlement
  direction `应缴 / 无需补退 / 应退`; legacy `已核定` is not a user-selectable
  filing result.
- The UI renders one handling amount and one process status. System estimates,
  official payable amounts, paid amounts, refundable amounts, and refunded
  amounts must not become duplicate list columns or duplicate summary cards.
- Payable and refundable results support partial registrations until their
  outstanding amount reaches zero. Every registration requires its own receipt.
  Payment registration atomically creates one `税费缴纳` pending-review payment
  row; refund registration atomically creates one `税费退还` pending-review
  receipt row. Refund rows do not count toward linked payment totals and the
  refund category is excluded from operating-income aggregation.
- Paid filings remain correctable, but cannot be voided. Correcting an earlier
  IIT month reopens later filed IIT months because cumulative withholding must
  be recalculated in order.
- 2026 quarterly mock deadlines use the official extended filing calendar; Q1
  filing and payment deadlines are `2026-04-20` rather than `2026-04-15`.
- Current automated evidence is refreshed by the 2026-07-13 tax refund cash-flow
  closure run. Backend persistence, cross-session idempotency, permissions, and
  database transactions remain outside this frontend-only evidence.

## 11. Personal Income Tax Actual-Payment Acceptance

- Salary confirmation validates the salary result but must not advance cumulative withholding history or enter IIT declaration totals.
- Only an immutable actual-payment record can advance cumulative withholding. It must freeze payroll month, actual payment date, IIT tax period, gross/net amount, withholding result, actor, and payroll number.
- IIT declaration preparation consumes payment records by actual-payment month. A June salary paid on July 15 belongs to June payroll reporting and July IIT declaration preparation.
- Salary confirmation and salary adjustment use the salary-attribution month for closed-period guards. Actual payment uses the actual-payment month; a closed salary month does not block a later open-period payment.
- Paid salary cannot be adjusted or reconfirmed. A deduction-profile update can reopen confirmed but unpaid salary, but must not mutate an existing payment snapshot.
- Tax-exempt and after-tax salary adjustments may affect gross/net payment but must not increase taxable wage income; taxable adjustments must.
- Frontend acceptance requires unit tests, root typecheck, root lint, web production build, AI Native adapter check, static legacy-contract scan, and `git diff --check`.
- This remains frontend-session evidence. Real bank payment execution, backend persistence, idempotency, authorization, and official natural-person tax filing are not claimed; the frontend finance-payment linkage is covered by the shared payable registration and finance-review workflow.

## 12. Natural-Person Labor Settlement And IIT Acceptance

- NP-07 must execute through the same production frontend workflows used by `/finance/receivables-payables` and `/finance/review`; a pure calculation fixture is not sufficient evidence.
- A resident individual labor settlement freezes gross payable, expense deduction, taxable income, withholding IIT, bank net payment, person, tax period, settlement batch and task sources before payment registration.
- Registration creates one pending-review bank-net cash-flow row and one linked confirmation-item progress update from a single validated workflow result. Pending-review rows do not settle the gross payable and do not enter IIT preparation.
- Review posting settles the confirmation item by the labor gross amount while the cash-flow remains the bank net amount. The difference must equal the frozen withholding IIT.
- Payment-month IIT preparation must include posted labor facts and must exclude unpaid or pending-review facts.
- Missing or inconsistent labor income snapshots must block both registration and review without partial frontend state writes. Historical posted natural-person payments without an income fact must remain visible as preparation blockers and must not be silently omitted.
- `naturalPersonPaymentFrontendWorkflow.test.ts` is the named NP-07 frontend-chain evidence. `financeNaturalPersonPaymentModel.test.ts` remains supporting model evidence.
- Pending-review labor payments must not generate accounting vouchers. After review posting, the shared accounting-voucher state must derive exactly one stable `待记账` funds voucher from the posted transaction.
- The labor voucher must debit the gross payable and credit bank net payment plus `2221.03` withheld IIT; repeated state derivation must keep one stable voucher identity rather than duplicating the candidate.
- `naturalPersonPaymentFrontendWorkflow.test.ts` is also the named NP-16 frontend-chain evidence. `financeNaturalPersonPaymentModel.test.ts` remains supporting entry-calculation evidence.
- Labor-payment reversal must be registered as a separate pending-review bank-net transaction. Pending reversal must not change settlement, IIT preparation, or accounting state.
- Review-posted labor reversal must restore the full gross payable, remove the unfiled labor fact, withdraw a source-empty unconfirmed IIT preparation, and derive one balanced adjustment voucher that debits bank net payment plus withheld IIT and credits gross labor payable.
- A second pending or posted reversal for the same labor payment must be rejected before frontend state mutation. `naturalPersonPaymentFrontendWorkflow.test.ts` is the named NP-14 frontend-chain evidence; the receivable/payable, natural-person-payment and tax-matter suites remain supporting model evidence.
- This remains in-memory frontend workflow evidence. Backend persistence, database transactions, idempotency, concurrency, authorization and official filing are not claimed.
## Task Customer Receivable Frontend Acceptance

The task customer receivable hardcut is tracked by TR-01 through TR-31 in
`docs/testing/task-customer-receivable-frontend-acceptance-traceability.md`.

Automated coverage is split by ownership:

- brand price resolution, contract eligibility, and version replacement;
- task snapshot freezing, acceptance facts, idempotency, month grouping, and contract cap;
- finance receivable adaptation, historical snapshots, late-fact issues, and non-overwrite behavior;
- end-to-end frontend consumption by invoice association, collection registration, accrual voucher creation, and independent part-time labor payable.

Browser evidence must cover brand pricing configuration, contract UI non-change,
employee price invisibility, and finance source traceability. Build/typecheck passing
alone is not acceptance evidence. Backend persistence and concurrency remain outside
this frontend test scope.
